# 機構成本下的六情境回測：台股多空、台指期疊加與現股當沖 / Six Trading Scenarios under Institution Costs: Stock Long-Short, TX Overlay and Day Trade

## 中文

### 1. 目的與結論

本報告記錄[實驗協定 §13.19](experiment_protocol.md) 的資訊性分析：同一組殘差標籤模型（B0 `t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a`、D0 `t-xs-scratch` 三 seed 集成）在**機構成本**（手續費 0.03%、滑價 0.05%、借券年費 2%）與**散戶成本**（手續費 0.0855%、滑價 0.1–0.2%、融券手續費 0.08%）下，六個多空情境的淨表現並列。前面 §13.14 至 §13.17 的判定都以散戶成本為準，結論是排序能力被成本吃掉；這裡回答的問題是：換成業界的成本條件，這個模型對業界有沒有吸引力。「吸引力」的門檻事前寫死：機構主要成本、分數位置、P1 ∪ P2 的淨 Sharpe ≥ 1.0、淨報酬 95% 區間下界 > 0、且 P2 單獨淨報酬 > 0。

結論：

1. **六個情境、兩個模型、三個市場共 36 格，沒有一格達標。** 最接近的是 D0 的 S2（多日多空加台指期滾動 beta 對沖）在兩市場合併：P1 ∪ P2 年化淨報酬 +16.8% [+1.4%, +33.7%]、Sharpe 0.83，區間下界過了 0，但 Sharpe 不到 1.0，P2 單獨 −6.5%。TPEX 的 D0 S2（Sharpe 0.87，P2 −7.3%）與 S6（Sharpe 0.86，P2 −9.7%）同樣只差 Sharpe 與 P2 兩項。
2. **機構成本不是多日多空的瓶頸。** S1 的成本拖累由散戶的 9.6–17%／年降到機構的 8–13%／年，差距只有 2–5 個百分點，但 P1 ∪ P2 的淨報酬仍為 B0 TWSE −6.3%、TPEX −2.5%，D0 TWSE −5.0%、TPEX +7.6%，P2 全部為負（−26% 到 −63%）。問題在空頭腿：P1 ∪ P2 多頭腿年化 +24% 到 +31%，空頭腿 −24% 到 −31%；P2 空頭腿 −61% 到 −89%，對上同期 TAIEX 報酬指數 +88.5%、TPEX +81.0%。第 3.7 節量了空頭腿實際持有的股票：規模與宇宙相同（成交金額百分位 0.52–0.55，宇宙 0.50），但進場前 20 日平均已漲 6–9%、60 日 17–26%（宇宙 1–4%、7–10%），多頭腿則是近期沒漲的股票；也就是說，模型的 h = 10 排序本質上是**短期反轉訊號**：放空近期強勢股、買進落後股。P1 這套反轉成立（空頭腿事後報酬低於宇宙、多頭腿高於宇宙），P2 是動能延續的行情：被放空的強勢股事後年化 +75% 到 +79%（宇宙 +57.5%），被買進的落後股只有 +21% 到 +27%，兩腿同時逆向。這與 §13.16「變體 A 讓 P2 排序不崩」並不矛盾：變體 A 恢復的是**全宇宙的殘差目標 Rank IC**（P2 0.05–0.065，一個相關係數），但第 3.7 節第二張表顯示，band 組合實際進場的前後 50 檔在 P2 的殘差目標價差已是 −0.4%／10 日（P1 +0.9% 到 +1.2%），而組合賺的是原始報酬，P2 的原始多空價差 −0.8% 到 −1.4%／10 日：殘差化只從**標籤**裡拿掉了市場、規模與產業，沒有拿掉動能，也沒有改變**組合**的暴露。模型的多空價差（毛年化 P1 ∪ P2 TWSE +1.8%／+7.9%）付不起任何成本檔。
3. **台指期疊加救回的是 beta，不是 alpha。** 多空組合對市場的滾動 beta 平均 −0.43 到 −0.52（空頭腿的 beta 高於多頭腿），對沖因此是**做多**台指期；TX 腿在 P1 ∪ P2 貢獻 +10% 到 +21%、P2 貢獻 +19% 到 +58%，把 S2 推到 B0 TWSE +13.5%、D0 TWSE +16.3%、D0 TW +16.8%。這一段報酬來自多頭市場裡持有約半倍市場曝險，任何人買 0050 都拿得到；拿掉 TX 腿後的 S1 就是上一點的結果。
4. **當沖是純粹的執行成本問題。** S3 的毛報酬年化 +123% 到 +179%（每邊每日 0.25–0.33%），h = 1 訊號真實且穩定（P2 毛報酬仍有 +122% 到 +142%）。機構成本的往返 0.31%／腿（手續費 0.06%、滑價 0.10%、當沖稅 0.15%）年化拖累 156%，淨報酬 TWSE B0 −32.6% [−55.5%, −8.4%]、D0 −17.4%，TPEX 轉正（B0 +7.4%、D0 +12.4%，Sharpe 0.33／0.53）但區間跨 0 且 P2 為負。散戶成本的拖累 263–363%／年，淨報酬 −119% 到 −224%，是六個情境裡最糟的。滑價歸零的機構敏感度（拖累 106%）下 TWSE P1 ∪ P2 淨報酬 B0 +17.2%（Sharpe 0.79）、D0 +32.4%（Sharpe 1.51）：當沖只有在每邊成交成本只剩手續費與稅、完全沒有滑價時才成立，而 0.15% 的當沖稅已占去機構往返成本的一半。
5. **合併情境沒有加分。** S5 把多日與當沖各半，Sharpe 介於兩者之間；S6 加上兩個 TX 疊加後 D0 TPEX +15.7% [−3.0%, +35.7%]、Sharpe 0.86，仍是靠 TX 腿，P2 −9.7%。
6. **容量把 TPEX 的正值抹掉。** 3 億與 10 億元在 5% 參與率上限下，多日組合 83–97% 的候選買單被擋，S1 的 10 億淨報酬 −18% 到 −33%；當沖的名目部署率降到 15–70%，TPEX 當沖 D0 3 億 +11.3% [−1.7%, +24.3%]（Sharpe 0.93）、10 億 +5.2%（部署率 14.5%）。有訊號的中小型股承載不了機構規模的資金。
7. **對業界的意義（原建構）。** 機構成本下的 Sharpe 區間是 0.5 到 0.9（含 beta 的 S2／S6），不含 beta 的 S1、S3 在 0 上下；P2 全部為負。原建構不是一個業者會付費的訊號：它需要（a）在 P2 這類動能延續的行情裡不被反轉式空頭腿拖垮的組合建構，（b）當沖每邊滑價壓到接近零的執行能力，（c）接受容量在 1 億元以下。
8. **補充實驗改變了多日多空的結論（第 3.8、3.9 節）。** 把分數對 60 日 beta 與前 20／60 日報酬每日正交化之後，同一組 band 參數的 S1 機構淨報酬由 −6% 到 +8% 變成 +11% 到 +29%，P2 全部轉正，最大回撤 −11% 到 −18%，組合 β 由 −0.5 降到 −0.2；B0 與 D0 的 S2 在三個市場、S1 在 TPEX 與 TW 共 10 格達到事前寫死的機構門檻，散戶成本下 B0 兩市場合併的 S1／S2 也為正。這個設計是在看過 P2 之後決定的，達標只在資訊層面，需 P3 確認；容量約 3 億元以內；空頭腿的可借券假設在 TPEX 最弱。

### 2. 資料與方法

| 項目 | 內容 |
| --- | --- |
| 預測 | B0 `launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736`（`t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a`）、D0 `launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215`（`t-xs-scratch`），皆為三 seed 集成、`residual_v1a` 標籤 |
| 價格與指數 | 資料集 raw `market.parquet`：還原開收盤價（報酬）、未調整價與成交量（流動性、整張）；TAIEX 與 TPEX 的 adjusted_close 為官方報酬指數（含息），表中「同期指數報酬」為該序列的年化 |
| 台指期 | 期交所 TX 日行情 2022-12 至 2026-06 的近月連續序列（到期月第三個星期三開盤換月、結算價 mark）；S2 用結算價對結算價、S4 用開盤到結算價 |
| 宇宙 | 每市場的流動性宇宙：20 日成交金額中位數 TWSE ≥ 3,000 萬、TPEX ≥ 1,000 萬，收盤 ≥ 10 元，排除 ETF；每日約 TWSE 490 檔、TPEX 355 檔；放空宇宙與多頭相同（假設皆可借券／融券／當沖先賣） |
| S1 多日多空 | 分數 → 市場內百分位 → 半衰期 3 日 EMA；多頭 band（進前 K、掉出前 m × K 賣、每日新進 ≤ ⌈K／10⌉）、空頭以反向排序套同一規則；漲停開盤不買、跌停開盤不賣，跌停開盤不放空、漲停開盤不回補；空頭位置每日等權重設，報酬 = −開盤到開盤報酬；多空各占資本 100% |
| S2／S4 TX 疊加 | 名目 = −β × 資本，β 為組合自身毛報酬對市場指數的滾動回歸係數（嚴格用 t 前 60 個區間，少於 30 個區間時為 0）；名目變動與換月（兩邊）計費 |
| S3 當沖 | t 收盤 h = 1 分數，t+1 開盤買前 q／放空後 q（每邊等權、各占資本 100%），t+1 收盤平倉；漲停開盤的多頭候選與跌停開盤的空頭候選跳過順延 |
| S5／S6 | 0.5 × 多日 + 0.5 × 當沖（各自含或不含 TX 疊加） |
| 格子與選擇 | S1：h ∈ {5, 10} × K ∈ {30, 50} × m ∈ {2, 3}；S3：q ∈ {5%, 10%}；以 TWSE、P1、機構主要成本、分數位置的淨 Sharpe 選定，B0：h = 10、K = 50、m = 3、q = 0.05；D0：h = 10、K = 50、m = 2、q = 0.05；選定後固定用於所有表 |
| 成本檔 | `backtest/config/cost_profiles.json`：散戶 手續費 0.0855%、證交稅 0.3%（當沖 0.15%）、滑價 TWSE 0.1%／TPEX 0.2%、融券手續費 0.08%、TX 每口 50 元加 1 點；機構 手續費 0.03%、證交稅同、滑價 0.05%、借券年費 2% 按日計、TX 每口 30 元加 1 點；滑價另報 0 與 2 倍 |
| 資本情境 | 分數位置（兩個成本檔）；機構另報 3 億與 10 億：多日每檔名目 > 20 日成交金額中位數 5% 的候選跳過，當沖每檔名目以該上限封頂、超出部分留現金 |
| 區間 | circular block bootstrap 1,000 次、seed 11；多日與合併區塊 21、當沖區塊 5；市場中性組合以 0 為基準 |
| 執行 | 本機 `backtest/`，`twbacktest.scenario_suite`，序列執行；輸出 `artifacts/diagnostics/backtest/scenario-suite-b0-d0-20261025T150000Z/` |

同期 TAIEX 開盤到開盤年化：P1 +21.9%、P2 +89.3%。空頭腿的規模與宇宙相同，不是大型股；它放空的是近 20–60 日漲幅最大的股票（反轉式空頭）。P1 的反轉成立：空頭腿事後報酬低於宇宙（B0 +14.4%、D0 +2.0% 對 +15.1%），多頭腿高於宇宙（+27% 對 +15%）。P2 動能延續：空頭腿事後 +75% 到 +79%、高於宇宙 17–21 個百分點，多頭腿 +21% 到 +27%、低於宇宙 30–37 個百分點，兩腿同時逆向。

兩腿進場的股票，其殘差目標（模型預測的量）與原始 alpha（對指數的 10 日相對報酬）的均值如下，每次進場計一次：

| 模型 | 期間 | 腿 | 進場次數 | 殘差目標 h = 10 均值 | 原始 alpha h = 10 均值 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B0 | P1 | 多頭 | 539 | +0.79% | +0.69% |
| B0 | P1 | 空頭 | 959 | −0.39% | −1.00% |
| B0 | P1 | 宇宙 | 242,925 | −0.01% | −0.67% |
| B0 | P2 | 多頭 | 139 | −0.21% | −2.45% |
| B0 | P2 | 空頭 | 346 | +0.24% | −1.05% |
| B0 | P2 | 宇宙 | 91,720 | −0.06% | −1.88% |
| D0 | P1 | 多頭 | 1,264 | +0.40% | +0.09% |
| D0 | P1 | 空頭 | 1,501 | −0.50% | −1.15% |
| D0 | P1 | 宇宙 | 242,925 | −0.01% | −0.67% |

| D0 | P2 | 空頭 | 543 | +0.22% | −1.38% |

多減空的 10 日價差：P1 殘差 +1.19%（B0）／+0.90%（D0），原始 +1.70%／+1.24%；P2 殘差 **−0.45%／−0.37%**，原始 **−1.40%／−0.76%**。§13.16 報告的「P2 殘差 Rank IC 0.05–0.065」是全宇宙的相關係數；在 band 組合實際進場的前後約 50 檔，P2 的殘差價差已經是負的，原始價差更負。殘差化從標籤拿掉的是市場、規模與產業，不是動能；組合持有的是原始股票，它的反動能傾斜在 P2 的動能行情裡付出的代價，是殘差 alpha 幅度的數倍。

#### 3.8 反轉暴露診斷（§13.19 補充；`twbacktest.reversal_exposure`）

把分數對結構性暴露純化之後，排序能力還剩多少？分數 V0 為 §13.14 的平滑百分位分數，V1 為每日在宇宙內把 V0 對前 20 日報酬、前 60 日報酬與 60 日 beta 做 OLS 的殘差。建構為每日成批、持有 10 日、前後四分位、金額中性（兩腿各占資本 100%，同 S1）：plain，或在前 20 日報酬的五分位內排序與配對（動能配平）。成本為每批兩腿各進出一次（機構約 25%／年、散戶 TWSE 約 36%、TPEX 約 44%）。

依 §13.19 補充第 6 項的事前規則：**(a) 成立於四個（模型, 市場）**。V1 動能配平在 P1 保留了 V0 plain 價差的 1.0 到 1.3 倍，P2 不再顯著為負（B0 TWSE −1.78% → +0.09%、B0 TPEX −1.46% → +0.82%、D0 TWSE −1.24% → +0.16%、D0 TPEX −0.54% → +0.91%），P1 ∪ P2 四格全部為正且區間不跨 0（TWSE +0.47%／+0.49%，TPEX +0.99%／+1.12% 每 10 日）。P2 的虧損主要是分數對 beta 與動能的結構性暴露，不是排序能力消失。(b) 不成立：價量模型不是只有反轉。(c) 週轉率條件化沒有一致效果：高跳升組的價差只在 B0 TPEX 的兩個期間都高於低跳升組，其他三組至少一個期間相反；免費的週轉率代理分不出會延續的強勢股。幅度對成本：純化後的年化毛價差 TWSE +12%、TPEX +25% 到 +28%，對每 10 日全數換手的機構成本（約 25%／年）仍是機構淨 TWSE −13%、TPEX −0.3% 到 +3%，散戶淨 −18% 到 −24%。純化讓訊號跨期穩定，沒有讓它變大；下一個可登記的實驗是把正交化分數與動能配平放進 band 建構（持有約 40 日、機構拖累 8–13%／年）重算 S1／S2。

#### 3.9 正交化分數的 band 建構（§13.19 補充二；`scenario_suite --config scenario_suite_v1.json`）

把 3.8 節的兩個處理放進 S1／S2 的 band 建構，h、K、m 固定為 §13.19 已選定的值（B0 10／50／3、D0 10／50／2，不重新選）。分數 V0 為原設定、V1 為每日對前 20／60 日報酬與 60 日 beta 取殘差；配平「無」為原建構、「動能五分位」為每個前 20 日報酬五分位各跑一對多空 band（K／5 個位置）等權合併。全部表格依 §13.19 格式，散戶與機構並列。

判讀：

1. **分數正交化（V1、無配平）就改變了結論。** 同一組 band 參數下，S1 的 P1 ∪ P2 機構淨報酬由 B0 TWSE −6.3% → +17.3% [−0.6%, +37.3%]（Sharpe −0.25 → 1.04）、B0 TPEX −2.5% → +19.4% [+4.9%, +34.4%]（1.50）、B0 TW −4.4% → +18.3% [+5.5%, +33.6%]（1.46）、D0 TPEX +7.6% → +28.8% [+13.5%, +43.6%]（2.16）、D0 TW +1.3% → +19.7% [+6.3%, +34.7%]（1.53）；P2 全部轉正（+4.7% 到 +23.2%），最大回撤由 −30% 到 −45% 縮到 −11% 到 −18%。組合的 β 由 −0.5 降到 −0.15 到 −0.20，TX 腿的貢獻只剩 +2% 到 +9%。
2. **達標的格子。** 機構成本下，B0 的 S1 在 TPEX 與 TW、S2 在三個市場，D0 的 S1 在 TPEX 與 TW、S2 在三個市場，共 **10 格達到事前寫死的門檻**（Sharpe ≥ 1.0、區間下界 > 0、P2 > 0）；B0 TWSE S1（下界 −0.6%）與 D0 TWSE S1（Sharpe 0.62）未達。散戶成本下 B0 TW 的 S1 +14.7% [+1.9%, +29.7%]（Sharpe 1.17）與 S2 +19.6% [+6.5%, +34.2%]（1.46）也為正。
3. **動能五分位配平在 band 建構裡失敗，原因是換手。** 持股換了五分位就離開子組合而被賣出，平均持有由 20 到 43 日掉到 4 日、每日換手 10%、成本拖累 20% 到 25%／年，P1 全部為負；這是補充二第 2 項事先寫明會計入的代價，結果證明以每日五分位重算的配平不可行。正交化單獨就把 β 與反動能傾斜拿掉，不需要配平。
4. **容量仍是上限。** V1 S1 在 3 億元：B0 TW +21.8%（Sharpe 1.71）、TPEX +28.0%（1.99）、D0 TW +9.0%；10 億元時 81% 到 97% 的候選買單被參與率上限擋掉，淨報酬 −8% 到 +11%。可行的規模約在 3 億元以內。
5. **這不是 P3 驗證。** 正交化本身沒有擬合參數（每日橫斷面 OLS），band 參數固定，但「對 beta 與動能正交化」這個設計是在看過 P2 失效之後才決定的；P2 的 +17% 到 +26% 因此不能當作樣本外證據，達標只在資訊層面，需要 P3（2026-06 之後）確認。另外，空頭腿假設流動性宇宙全部可借券，而最強的結果在 TPEX，正是借券最難的區段；S1 的絕對報酬（+17% 到 +29%）仍低於同期 TAIEX 報酬指數（+41%），它贏的是風險調整後的市場中性報酬，不是 0050 買進持有。

| D0 | TPEX | 10 | 30 | 2 | P1 | +56.4% | +38.1% | [+12.6%, +62.0%] | +2.57 | -12.7% | +6.8% | +18.3% | 14.4 |

依補充三第 4 項的事前規則：

- **(a) 逐年：B0 不靠單一年份，D0 的 TPEX 靠前兩年。** B0 V1 的 S1 在機構成本下四個曆年全部為正：TWSE +1.0%／+16.8%／+12.4%／+23.2%（2026 至 5 月），TPEX +24.4%／+22.5%／+3.0%／+12.7%，TW +12.3%／+20.0%／+7.9%／+18.1%，沒有一年貢獻超過總淨報酬的一半。D0 的 TWSE 在 2025 年為 −1.5%（2024 年 +25.8% 撐起大半），TPEX 由 2023 年 +48.4%、2024 年 +36.7% 掉到 2025 年 +12.7%、2026 年 +3.3%，前兩年占總淨報酬八成以上且逐年遞減；D0 的 band 參數是在 P1 選的，這個遞減可能是選擇效應，也可能是訊號衰減，P3 才分得出來。
- **(b) 全格子：鄰近參數同向。** V1 分數下 8 組 (h, K, m) 的 S1 在四個（模型, 市場）的 P1 ∪ P2 機構淨報酬全部為正（B0 TWSE +6.3% 到 +21.6%、B0 TPEX +16.4% 到 +27.9%、D0 TWSE +5.0% 到 +20.0%、D0 TPEX +26.9% 到 +43.6%），TPEX 的 16 組區間下界全部 > 0；P2 只有兩組為負（B0 TWSE h = 10、K = 50、m = 2 的 −1.6%，D0 TWSE h = 5、K = 50、m = 2 的 −12.4%），區間都跨 0。§13.19 選定的 (10, 50, 3)／(10, 50, 2) 在 V1 格子裡居中，不是最好的一組（TPEX 最好的是 h = 5、K = 30、m = 3；TWSE 是 h = 10、K = 30、m = 3），所以 3.9 節的結果沒有占到參數選擇的便宜。
- 兩項檢查都不改變 3.9 節第 5 點的限制：設計在看過 P2 後決定、空頭腿的可借券假設、絕對報酬低於同期指數。它們排除的是「靠一年」與「靠一組參數」這兩種最常見的假象。

### 4. 意涵

- 把成本換成機構條件，改變的是幅度不是結論：S1 的淨報酬只多出 2–5 個百分點，當沖的拖累由 263–363% 降到 156%，但毛報酬 123–179% 仍付不起。模型的排序能力在這套工具下換不成業界會買單的 Sharpe。
- 真正的瓶頸有兩個：空頭腿的建構（模型的多日排序是短期反轉訊號，P2 的空頭是在放空近期漲幅最大、且繼續漲的股票）與當沖的執行成本（0.15% 當沖稅加任何滑價）。兩者都不是模型層能解決的；前者需要空頭加動能或 beta 控制、或只做多加指數對沖，後者需要接近零滑價的執行。
- 含 TX 腿的 S2／S6 看起來最好（Sharpe 0.5–0.9），但那是半倍市場曝險在多頭市場的報酬；扣掉 TX 腿後就回到 S1／S5。報告不把它當成 alpha。
- 唯一淨報酬為正且 Sharpe 接近 1 的格子（D0 S3 TPEX 3 億，+11.3%，Sharpe 0.93）容量只有約 3 成部署率，區間跨 0、P2 為負。
- 依 §13.19 第 9 項，原建構未達標。補充二的正交化建構在機構成本下有 10 格達標，依協定改寫結論為「正交化後的多日多空在機構成本下達到資訊性門檻，待 P3 確認」，並登記 P3 前瞻（2026-06 之後，V1、無配平、固定參數、兩個成本檔）為下一個實驗。

### 5. 限制

- P2 已看過；參數在 P1 選、P1 ∪ P2 判讀含 P1。
- 機構成本（0.03%、0.05%、2%）是假設值，未查證實際報價；放空宇宙假設流動性宇宙全部可借，實際借券供給會縮小空頭腿；當沖假設全部可先賣後買。
- 空頭位置以每日等權重設近似；借券費以年率除以 252 按日扣。
- TX 的滾動 β 以組合自身 60 個區間估計，是歷史 beta；S2 用結算價對結算價、現股用開盤到開盤，含約 0.3% 的時點噪音。
- 3 億與 10 億的參與率上限是硬性篩選與封頂，不是市場衝擊模型。
- TW 合併是兩市場各半的簡單平均，不是跨市場聯合排序。

### 6. 重現方式

```bash
cd backtest
uv run python -m twbacktest.scenario_suite --predictions ../artifacts/lgbm/launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736/predictions.parquet --model t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a --predictions ../artifacts/xs/launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215/predictions.parquet --model t-xs-scratch --prices ../artifacts/datasets/<dataset>/raw/market.parquet
uv run python -m twbacktest.reversal_exposure --predictions ../artifacts/lgbm/launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736/predictions.parquet --model t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a --targets ../artifacts/lgbm/launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736/targets.parquet --predictions ../artifacts/xs/launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215/predictions.parquet --model t-xs-scratch --targets ../artifacts/xs/launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215/targets.parquet --prices ../artifacts/datasets/<dataset>/raw/market.parquet
uv run python -m twbacktest.scenario_suite --config config/scenario_suite_v1.json --predictions ... --model ... --prices ...
uv run python -m twbacktest.scenario_suite --config config/scenario_suite_v1_grid.json --predictions ... --model ... --prices ...
python3 scripts/build_scenario_report.py docs/institution_cost_scenarios_results.md reports/institution_cost_scenarios_20261025.docx
```

## English

### 1. Purpose and conclusions

This report records the informational analysis of [experiment protocol §13.19](experiment_protocol.md): the same residual-label models (B0 `t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a` and the three-seed D0 ensemble `t-xs-scratch`) evaluated in six long-short scenarios under **institution costs** (commission 0.03%, slippage 0.05%, borrow fee 2% a year) side by side with **retail costs** (commission 0.0855%, slippage 0.1–0.2%, margin-short fee 0.08%). Every decision from §13.14 to §13.17 used retail costs and concluded that the ranking skill is eaten by costs; the question here is whether the model becomes attractive to the industry under the industry's cost conditions. The bar was fixed before scoring: institution primary costs, fractional positions, P1 ∪ P2 net Sharpe ≥ 1.0, net-return 95% lower bound > 0 and a positive P2 net return.

Conclusions:

1. **None of the 36 cells (six scenarios × two models × three markets) clears the bar.** The closest is D0's S2 (multi-day long-short plus a rolling-beta TX overlay) on the two markets combined: P1 ∪ P2 net +16.8% [+1.4%, +33.7%] a year, Sharpe 0.83, lower bound above 0, but Sharpe below 1.0 and P2 at −6.5%. D0's S2 (Sharpe 0.87, P2 −7.3%) and S6 (Sharpe 0.86, P2 −9.7%) on TPEX miss the same two conditions.
2. **Institution costs are not the bottleneck of the multi-day long-short.** S1's cost drag falls from 9.6–17% a year (retail) to 8–13% (institution), a 2–5 point difference, yet the P1 ∪ P2 net return stays at B0 TWSE −6.3%, TPEX −2.5%, D0 TWSE −5.0%, TPEX +7.6%, with every P2 negative (−26% to −63%). The short book is the problem: on P1 ∪ P2 the long book earns +24% to +31% a year and the short book −24% to −31%; in P2 the short book loses 61% to 89% while the TAIEX total-return index gains 88.5% and TPEX 81.0%. Section 3.7 measures what the short book actually held: names of the same size as the universe (dollar-volume percentile 0.52–0.55 against 0.50) that had already risen 6–9% over the previous 20 sessions and 17–26% over 60 (universe 1–4% and 7–10%), while the long book held names that had not moved. The model's h = 10 ranking is therefore in substance a **short-term reversal signal**: short the recent winners, buy the laggards. In P1 the reversal worked (the shorts then underperformed the universe and the longs outperformed it); P2 was a momentum regime in which the shorted winners went on to earn +75% to +79% a year (universe +57.5%) and the bought laggards only +21% to +27%, so both legs ran against the book at once. This does not contradict §13.16's finding that variant A keeps the P2 ranking alive: variant A restored the **universe-wide Rank IC against the residual target** (P2 0.05–0.065, a correlation coefficient), but the second table of section 3.7 shows that for the top and bottom 50 names the band portfolio actually entered, the P2 residual-target spread is already −0.4% per 10 sessions (P1 +0.9% to +1.2%), and the portfolio earns raw returns, whose P2 long-short spread is −0.8% to −1.4% per 10 sessions: residualization removed market, size and industry from the **labels**, not momentum, and it changed nothing about the **portfolio's** exposures. The model's long-short spread (gross P1 ∪ P2 TWSE +1.8% / +7.9%) cannot pay for either cost profile.
3. **The TX overlay recovers beta, not alpha.** The long-short book's rolling beta averages −0.43 to −0.52 (the shorts carry more beta than the longs), so the hedge is **long** TX; the TX leg contributes +10% to +21% on P1 ∪ P2 and +19% to +58% in P2, lifting S2 to B0 TWSE +13.5%, D0 TWSE +16.3% and D0 TW +16.8%. That return is half a unit of market exposure in a bull market, available to anyone holding 0050; take the TX leg away and S1 is what remains.
4. **Day trading is purely an execution-cost problem.** S3 earns +123% to +179% gross a year (0.25–0.33% per side per session); the h = 1 signal is real and stable (P2 gross still +122% to +142%). The institution round trip of 0.31% per leg (commission 0.06%, slippage 0.10%, day-trade tax 0.15%) costs 156% a year: net TWSE B0 −32.6% [−55.5%, −8.4%], D0 −17.4%; TPEX turns positive (B0 +7.4%, D0 +12.4%, Sharpe 0.33 / 0.53) but the intervals span 0 and P2 is negative. Retail costs drag 263–363% a year for net returns of −119% to −224%, the worst of the six scenarios. With institution slippage set to zero (drag 106%) the TWSE P1 ∪ P2 net is B0 +17.2% (Sharpe 0.79) and D0 +32.4% (Sharpe 1.51): day trading works only when each side costs nothing beyond commission and tax, and the 0.15% day-trade tax alone is half of the institution round trip.
5. **Combining the books adds nothing.** S5 (half multi-day, half day trade) lands between its parts; S6 with both TX overlays reaches D0 TPEX +15.7% [−3.0%, +35.7%], Sharpe 0.86, again through the TX leg, with P2 at −9.7%.
6. **Capacity erases the TPEX positives.** At NT$300M and NT$1B under the 5% participation cap, 83–97% of the multi-day candidate buys are blocked and S1's NT$1B net is −18% to −33%; the day-trade deployed share falls to 15–70%, with TPEX D0 at NT$300M +11.3% [−1.7%, +24.3%] (Sharpe 0.93) and at NT$1B +5.2% (14.5% deployed). The small and mid caps that carry the signal cannot absorb institutional capital.
7. **What this means for the industry (original construction).** Under institution costs the Sharpe range is 0.5 to 0.9 for the beta-carrying S2 / S6 and around 0 for S1 / S3; every P2 is negative. The original construction is not a signal a firm would pay for. It would need (a) a short-book construction that survives a momentum regime like P2 without being dragged down by a reversal-driven short book, (b) execution that brings day-trade slippage to nearly zero, and (c) acceptance of capacity below NT$100M.
8. **The supplementary experiments change the conclusion for the multi-day long-short (sections 3.8 and 3.9).** After orthogonalizing the score every day against the 60-session beta and the trailing 20 / 60-session returns, S1's institution net with the same band parameters moves from −6% to +8% to +11% to +29%, every P2 turns positive, the maximum drawdown shrinks to −11% to −18% and the book's β falls from −0.5 to −0.2; B0's and D0's S2 on all three markets and S1 on TPEX and TW, 10 cells in all, clear the pre-registered institution bar, and under retail costs B0's S1 / S2 on the two markets combined are positive as well. The design was chosen after seeing P2, so the bar is met at the informational level only and needs P3; capacity is about NT$300M or less; the borrowability assumption of the short book is weakest on TPEX.

### 2. Data and method

| Item | Content |
| --- | --- |
| Predictions | B0 `launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736` (`t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a`), D0 `launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215` (`t-xs-scratch`), three-seed ensembles on the `residual_v1a` labels |
| Prices and indexes | Dataset raw `market.parquet`: adjusted opens and closes (returns), raw prices and volume (liquidity, board lots); the adjusted_close of TAIEX and TPEX is the official total-return index, annualized in the "Index TR" column |
| TX | TAIFEX daily TX prices 2022-12 to 2026-06, continuous front month (rolled at the open of the third Wednesday, settlement mark); S2 uses settlement to settlement, S4 open to settlement |
| Universe | Per-market liquidity universe: 20-session median dollar volume ≥ NT$30M (TWSE) / NT$10M (TPEX), close ≥ NT$10, no ETFs; about 490 TWSE and 355 TPEX names a day; the short universe equals the long one (every name assumed borrowable / shortable / day-tradable) |
| S1 multi-day L/S | Score → within-market percentile → EMA (half-life 3); long band (buy into the top K, sell out of the top m × K, at most ⌈K / 10⌉ entries a day), the short book runs the same rule on the reversed ranking; no buys at a limit-up open, no sales at a limit-down open, no short sales at a limit-down open, no covers at a limit-up open; each short slot reset to its value every session with return = minus the open-to-open move; long and short books each carry 100% of the capital |
| S2 / S4 TX overlay | Notional = −β × capital, β the rolling regression coefficient of the book's own gross return on the market index (strictly the 60 intervals before t, 0 with fewer than 30); fees on notional changes and rolls (both sides) |
| S3 day trade | h = 1 scores at the close of t; buy the top q and sell short the bottom q at the open of t+1 (equal weights, each leg 100% of capital), close at that session's close; long candidates at a limit-up open and short candidates at a limit-down open are skipped |
| S5 / S6 | 0.5 × multi-day + 0.5 × day trade, with or without the TX overlays |
| Grid and selection | S1: h ∈ {5, 10} × K ∈ {30, 50} × m ∈ {2, 3}; S3: q ∈ {5%, 10%}; selected by net Sharpe on TWSE, P1, institution primary costs, fractional positions: B0 h = 10, K = 50, m = 3, q = 0.05; D0 h = 10, K = 50, m = 2, q = 0.05; fixed for every other table |
| Cost profiles | `backtest/config/cost_profiles.json`: retail commission 0.0855%, transaction tax 0.3% (day trade 0.15%), slippage TWSE 0.1% / TPEX 0.2%, margin-short fee 0.08%, TX NT$50 per contract plus one point; institution commission 0.03%, same tax, slippage 0.05%, borrow fee 2% a year charged per session, TX NT$30 plus one point; slippage also at 0 and 2× |
| Capital scenarios | Fractional (both profiles); institution also NT$300M and NT$1B: multi-day candidates whose slot exceeds 5% of the 20-session median dollar volume are skipped, day-trade slots are capped at that limit with the excess left in cash |
| Intervals | Circular block bootstrap, 1,000 replicates, seed 11; block 21 for multi-day and combined series, 5 for day trade; market-neutral books measured against 0 |
| Run | Local `backtest/`, `twbacktest.scenario_suite`, serial; output `artifacts/diagnostics/backtest/scenario-suite-b0-d0-20261025T150000Z/` |

#### 3.7 What the two S1 books actually held (TWSE, h = 10, K = 50, selected m)

Computed from the daily holdings (`held`) of `simulate_band`: size is the percentile of the 20-session median dollar volume inside the universe on the signal date (1 = largest), "before entry" is the log return over the 20 / 60 sessions before the signal date, and "afterwards" is the mean daily open-to-open return while held (× 252).

| D0 | P2 | Universe | 426.6 | 0.50 | 25% | +3.8% | +9.5% | +57.5% |

TAIEX open to open over the same intervals: P1 +21.9%, P2 +89.3% annualized. The short book has the universe's size, not large caps; what it shorts are the names with the largest gains over the previous 20–60 sessions (a reversal short). In P1 the reversal held: the shorts then earned less than the universe (B0 +14.4%, D0 +2.0% against +15.1%) and the longs more (+27% against +15%). In P2 momentum persisted: the shorts earned +75% to +79%, 17–21 points above the universe, and the longs +21% to +27%, 30–37 points below it, so both legs ran against the book.

The mean residual target (what the model predicts) and raw alpha (10-session return relative to the index) of the names each book entered, one count per entry:

| Model | Period | Book | Entries | Mean residual target h = 10 | Mean raw alpha h = 10 |

| B0 | P1 | Long | 539 | +0.79% | +0.69% |

| B0 | P1 | Universe | 242,925 | −0.01% | −0.67% |

| B0 | P2 | Short | 346 | +0.24% | −1.05% |
| B0 | P2 | Universe | 91,720 | −0.06% | −1.88% |

Long-minus-short spread per 10 sessions: P1 residual +1.19% (B0) / +0.90% (D0), raw +1.70% / +1.24%; P2 residual **−0.45% / −0.37%**, raw **−1.40% / −0.76%**. The "P2 residual Rank IC 0.05–0.065" of the §13.16 report is a universe-wide correlation; among the roughly 50 names at each end that the band portfolio actually enters, the P2 residual spread is already negative and the raw spread more so. Residualization took market, size and industry out of the labels, not momentum; the portfolio holds raw stocks, and the price its anti-momentum tilt paid in P2's momentum regime is several times the size of the residual alpha.

#### 3.8 Reversal-exposure diagnostic (§13.19 supplement; `twbacktest.reversal_exposure`)

How much ranking skill is left once the score is purified of its structural exposures? Score V0 is the §13.14 smoothed percentile score; V1 is its daily OLS residual inside the universe on the trailing 20-session return, the trailing 60-session return and a 60-session beta. Constructions are daily cohorts held 10 sessions, top-minus-bottom quartile, dollar-neutral with each leg at full capital (as in S1): plain, or ranked and paired inside quintiles of the trailing 20-session return (momentum-matched). Costs are one entry and exit per leg per cohort (institution about 25% a year, retail about 36% on TWSE and 44% on TPEX).

The raw score correlates −0.24 to −0.45 with the 60-session beta (more strongly in P2) and −0.10 to −0.26 with the trailing 20 / 60-session returns: the model ranks low-beta names that have not moved at the top, which is where the S1 book's β ≈ −0.5 and its anti-momentum tilt come from. After orthogonalization every V1 correlation is within ±0.08.

Under the pre-registered rules of §13.19 supplement item 6: **(a) holds for all four (model, market) pairs**. V1 momentum-matched keeps 1.0 to 1.3 times the V0 plain spread in P1 and is no longer significantly negative in P2 (B0 TWSE −1.78% → +0.09%, B0 TPEX −1.46% → +0.82%, D0 TWSE −1.24% → +0.16%, D0 TPEX −0.54% → +0.91%); on P1 ∪ P2 all four cells are positive with intervals clear of 0 (TWSE +0.47% / +0.49%, TPEX +0.99% / +1.12% per 10 sessions). The P2 loss is mostly the score's structural exposure to beta and momentum, not vanished ranking skill. (b) does not hold: the price-volume model is not reversal only. (c) Turnover conditioning has no consistent effect: the high-spike tercile beats the low one in both periods only for B0 on TPEX, and at least one period runs the other way in the other three cases; the free turnover proxy cannot tell which winners will keep running. Magnitude against costs: the purified gross spread is +12% a year on TWSE and +25% to +28% on TPEX, which against the institution cost of full turnover every 10 sessions (about 25% a year) still leaves TWSE −13% and TPEX −0.3% to +3% net, and retail −18% to −24%. Purification made the signal stable across regimes; it did not make it bigger. The next experiment worth registering is the orthogonalized score with momentum-matched legs inside the band construction (holding about 40 sessions, institution drag 8–13% a year), rerunning S1 / S2.

#### 3.9 Band construction on the orthogonalized score (§13.19 supplement 2; `scenario_suite --config scenario_suite_v1.json`)

The two treatments of section 3.8 placed inside the S1 / S2 band construction, with h, K, m fixed at the §13.19 selection (B0 10 / 50 / 3, D0 10 / 50 / 2, no re-selection). Score V0 is the original setting and V1 the daily residual on the trailing 20 / 60-session returns and the 60-session beta; matching "none" is the original construction and "momentum quintiles" runs one long-short band pair per quintile of the trailing 20-session return (K / 5 slots each), combined with equal weights. Every table follows the §13.19 format with retail and institution side by side.

Reading:

1. **Orthogonalizing the score (V1, no matching) alone changes the conclusion.** With the same band parameters, S1's P1 ∪ P2 institution net goes from B0 TWSE −6.3% → +17.3% [−0.6%, +37.3%] (Sharpe −0.25 → 1.04), B0 TPEX −2.5% → +19.4% [+4.9%, +34.4%] (1.50), B0 TW −4.4% → +18.3% [+5.5%, +33.6%] (1.46), D0 TPEX +7.6% → +28.8% [+13.5%, +43.6%] (2.16), D0 TW +1.3% → +19.7% [+6.3%, +34.7%] (1.53); every P2 turns positive (+4.7% to +23.2%) and the maximum drawdown shrinks from −30% to −45% to −11% to −18%. The book's β falls from −0.5 to −0.15 to −0.20 and the TX leg contributes only +2% to +9%.
2. **Cells that clear the bar.** Under institution costs, B0's S1 on TPEX and TW and S2 on all three markets, and D0's S1 on TPEX and TW and S2 on all three markets, **10 cells in all**, clear the pre-registered bar (Sharpe ≥ 1.0, lower bound > 0, P2 > 0); B0 TWSE S1 (lower bound −0.6%) and D0 TWSE S1 (Sharpe 0.62) do not. Under retail costs B0 TW's S1 +14.7% [+1.9%, +29.7%] (Sharpe 1.17) and S2 +19.6% [+6.5%, +34.2%] (1.46) are positive as well.
3. **Momentum-quintile matching fails inside the band construction, because of turnover.** A holding that changes quintile leaves its sub-book's ranking and is sold: the mean holding falls from 20–43 to 4 sessions, turnover rises to 10% a day and the cost drag to 20–25% a year, and every P1 is negative. Supplement 2 item 2 said this cost would be counted; the result shows that matching by daily re-computed quintiles is not viable. Orthogonalization alone removes the β and anti-momentum tilt, so the matching is not needed.
4. **Capacity remains the ceiling.** V1 S1 at NT$300M: B0 TW +21.8% (Sharpe 1.71), TPEX +28.0% (1.99), D0 TW +9.0%; at NT$1B 81–97% of the candidate buys are blocked by the participation cap and the net is −8% to +11%. The workable scale is about NT$300M or less.
5. **This is not a P3 validation.** The orthogonalization has no fitted parameters (a daily cross-sectional OLS) and the band parameters are fixed, but the decision to orthogonalize against beta and momentum was taken after seeing P2 fail on momentum, so P2's +17% to +26% cannot count as out-of-sample evidence; the bar is met at the informational level only and needs P3 (after 2026-06). The short book also assumes every liquid name is borrowable, and the strongest results are on TPEX, exactly where borrowing is hardest; S1's absolute return (+17% to +29%) is still below the TAIEX total-return index over the same period (+41%), so what it wins is a risk-adjusted market-neutral return, not a beat of 0050 buy-and-hold.

Under the pre-registered rules of supplement 3 item 4:

- **(a) Year by year: B0 does not rest on one year; D0's TPEX rests on the first two.** B0's V1 S1 under institution costs is positive in every calendar year: TWSE +1.0% / +16.8% / +12.4% / +23.2% (2026 to May), TPEX +24.4% / +22.5% / +3.0% / +12.7%, TW +12.3% / +20.0% / +7.9% / +18.1%, with no single year contributing more than half of the total. D0's TWSE is −1.5% in 2025 (2024's +25.8% carries most of the total) and its TPEX falls from +48.4% in 2023 and +36.7% in 2024 to +12.7% in 2025 and +3.3% in 2026: the first two years hold more than 80% of the total and the series decays year by year. D0's band parameters were selected on P1, so the decay may be a selection effect or a fading signal; only P3 can tell them apart.
- **(b) The grid: neighbouring parameters point the same way.** On the V1 score all 8 (h, K, m) combinations give a positive P1 ∪ P2 institution net for S1 in every (model, market) pair (B0 TWSE +6.3% to +21.6%, B0 TPEX +16.4% to +27.9%, D0 TWSE +5.0% to +20.0%, D0 TPEX +26.9% to +43.6%), and all 16 TPEX lower bounds are above 0; only two P2 cells are negative (B0 TWSE h = 10, K = 50, m = 2 at −1.6%; D0 TWSE h = 5, K = 50, m = 2 at −12.4%), both with intervals spanning 0. The §13.19 selection (10, 50, 3) / (10, 50, 2) sits mid-grid on V1, not at the top (TPEX's best is h = 5, K = 30, m = 3; TWSE's is h = 10, K = 30, m = 3), so section 3.9 did not benefit from the parameter choice.
- Neither check removes the limits of section 3.9 item 5: the design was chosen after seeing P2, the short book assumes borrowability, and the absolute return trails the index. What they rule out are the two commonest artefacts, a single strong year and a single lucky parameter set.

### 4. Implications

- Switching to institution costs changes magnitudes, not the conclusion: S1 gains only 2–5 points of net return, and the day-trade drag falls from 263–363% to 156% while the 123–179% gross still cannot pay it. With these instruments the model's ranking skill does not turn into a Sharpe the industry would buy.
- Two bottlenecks remain: the construction of the short book (the multi-day ranking is a short-term reversal signal; in P2 it shorts the names that rose most recently and kept rising) and the execution cost of day trading (the 0.15% day-trade tax plus any slippage). Neither is a model-layer problem; the first needs momentum or beta controls on the shorts or long-only with an index hedge, the second needs near-zero slippage.
- S2 / S6 with the TX leg look best (Sharpe 0.5–0.9), but that is half a unit of market exposure in a bull market; without the TX leg they fall back to S1 / S5. The report does not count it as alpha.
- The only cell with a positive net return and a Sharpe near 1 (D0 S3 TPEX at NT$300M, +11.3%, Sharpe 0.93) deploys about a third of its notional, its interval spans 0 and its P2 is negative.
- Under §13.19 item 9 the original construction does not meet the bar. The orthogonalized construction of supplement 2 clears it in 10 cells under institution costs, so the protocol's conclusion is rewritten to "the orthogonalized multi-day long-short meets the informational bar under institution costs, pending P3", and a P3 forward test (after 2026-06, V1, no matching, fixed parameters, both cost profiles) is registered as the next experiment.

### 5. Limits

- P2 has been seen; parameters are selected on P1 and the P1 ∪ P2 reading includes P1.
- The institution costs (0.03%, 0.05%, 2%) are assumptions, not quotes; the short universe assumes every liquid name is borrowable, which overstates the short book; day trading assumes every name can be sold short first.
- Short positions are approximated by daily equal-weight resets; the borrow fee is the annual rate divided by 252 per session.
- The TX rolling β is estimated from the book's own 60 intervals and is a historical beta; S2 marks TX settlement to settlement while stocks run open to open, about 0.3% of timing noise.
- The NT$300M and NT$1B participation caps are hard filters and caps, not a market-impact model.
- The TW combination is a simple half-and-half average of the two markets, not a joint cross-market ranking.

### 6. Reproduction

```bash
cd backtest
uv run python -m twbacktest.scenario_suite --predictions ../artifacts/lgbm/launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736/predictions.parquet --model t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a --predictions ../artifacts/xs/launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215/predictions.parquet --model t-xs-scratch --prices ../artifacts/datasets/<dataset>/raw/market.parquet
uv run python -m twbacktest.reversal_exposure --predictions ../artifacts/lgbm/launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736/predictions.parquet --model t-lgbm-alpha-residual_v1a --targets ../artifacts/lgbm/launch-20261023T103805Z-2637116736/targets.parquet --predictions ../artifacts/xs/launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215/predictions.parquet --model t-xs-scratch --targets ../artifacts/xs/launch-20261023T162203Z-877225215/targets.parquet --prices ../artifacts/datasets/<dataset>/raw/market.parquet
uv run python -m twbacktest.scenario_suite --config config/scenario_suite_v1.json --predictions ... --model ... --prices ...
uv run python -m twbacktest.scenario_suite --config config/scenario_suite_v1_grid.json --predictions ... --model ... --prices ...
python3 scripts/build_scenario_report.py docs/institution_cost_scenarios_results.md reports/institution_cost_scenarios_20261025.docx
```
